Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a biophysics algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 27. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 756/3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_27(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 27. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^756(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)
